- **customer-evidence**: Mine customer feedback into a ranked, segmented list of problems with verbatims — app-store and review-site reviews, NPS comments, leaving/cancellation surveys, support tickets, sales-call notes, forum threads and interview transcripts. - **customer-interview-synthesis**: Prepare a synthesis of customer call notes that answers one question in the customers' own words. Every quotation is exact and cited to its note, with the date, the speaker's role, the permission given and whether the words came unprompted.
